ultra:~# echo BRAVE-BROKER; cat /proc/1634/status; echo ns_user=$(readlink /proc
/1634/ns/user); echo ns_pid=$(readlink /proc/1634/ns/pid); echo BRAVE-BROKER-FIN
; for p in /proc/[0-9]*; do args=$(tr "\0" " " < "$p/cmdline" 2>/dev/null); case
 "$args" in /opt/brave/brave*--type=renderer*) echo BRAVE-RENDERER; cat "$p/stat
us"; echo ns_user=$(readlink "$p/ns/user"); echo ns_pid=$(readlink "$p/ns/pid");
 cat "$p/uid_map"; echo BRAVE-RENDERER-FIN;; esac; done; echo BRAVE-CRED-LISTO-$
